# Grant Opportunities

#### The MHN has compiled a collection of grant opportunities that are available to support existing services and creation of new programs. Grant Opportunities are updated on a monthly basis. Regularly visit this page for new funding opportunities.

## Funding Support Offerings

###### Open Grants & Funding List

The latest funding opportunities in the field of maternal and women's health.

Browse the list

###### Recurring Grants & Giving

A compilation of recurring grants from foundations and corporations

Browse the list

Request a letter

## NEW!!! 2026 Bridget “Biddy” Mason Scholarship & Financial Aid Fund

Applications are now open for the 2026 Bridget “Biddy” Mason Scholarship & Financial Aid Fund, sponsored by Californians for the Advancement of Midwifery and administered by the Birthland Wellness Fund. The Fund supports Black midwifery students and newly licensed midwives by reducing financial barriers along the journey from education to practice. Funding opportunities include an educational scholarship, licensing fee assistance, and practice start-up capital.

###### Funding Available:

###### Due Date:

###### Funding Source:

Funding amount not specified.

## NEW!!! TEEEM Foundation Community Impact Grant

TEEEM Foundation is a global nonprofit and charitable organization that creates sustainable, community-driven solutions in education, youth development, health, empowerment, and entrepreneurship. Three funding tiers are available: Community Grants ($5K–$10K), Growth Grants ($10K–$20K), and Transformational Grants ($20K–$30K). Organizations working with underserved communities and young people are especially encouraged to apply.

###### Funding Available:

###### Due Date:

###### Funding Source:

The Oak Foundation makes grants to nonprofit organizations working to create a safer, fairer, and more sustainable world. The foundation prioritizes initiatives that address root causes, are replicable, plan for long-term sustainability, and value community participation. Organizations may submit an unsolicited letter of inquiry if strong alignment exists with funding priorities. Applications accepted on a rolling basis.

## AJ Muste Foundation for Peace + Justice: Rapid Response Grants

The Muste Foundation provides grants, sponsorships, and educational resources to hundreds of grassroots projects around the world. We fund innovative organizing, often with seed funds that give a necessary boost to bold ideas. The Rapid Response Grants are for the immediate and/or emergency needs of groups involved in grassroots nonviolent activism. They are to help respond to unexpected needs and timely political opportunities with organizing events and direct actions.

## Ability Central

Ability Central is committed to supporting programs that expand communication access, advance accessibility, and foster inclusion. From assistive technology and communication supports to workforce development and community engagement, their investments help create meaningful opportunities for people with disabilities to participate, connect, learn, and thrive.

###### Funding Available:

Grants of up to $200,000 are available.

###### Focus Areas:

Disabilities & Accessibility

Learn more

## American Association of Birth Centers (AABC) Foundation

The AABC Foundation is investing in a brighter future of birth care— one grant, scholarship, and research initiative at a time. They raise funds to support the advancement of the AABC midwifery-led birth center model as a standard to achieve high-value, evidence-based wellness care that is equitable, safe, and respectful. The Foundation aims to accomplish this mission by identifying funding needs and appropriate resources necessary to carry out the following goals: Expansion, Education, Accessibility, Research, and Accreditation.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amounts vary.

###### Focus Areas:

Midwifery, Professional Development, Education, Research

Learn more

## Cardenas Markets Foundation

The Cardenas CARES Community Giving Program, offered through the Cardenas Markets Foundation, supports local nonprofit organizations through monetary and in-kind donations. This initiative is designed to uplift and invest in the communities where Cardenas Markets operates. Funding opportunities include monetary donations and in-kind donations.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amount not specified.

###### Focus Areas:

No specific focus areas.

Learn more

## Costco Wholesale Charitable Contributions

Costco Wholesale’s primary charitable efforts specifically focus on programs supporting children, education, and health and human services in the communities where they do business. Throughout the year, they receive a large number of requests from nonprofit organizations striving to make a positive impact, and are thankful to be able to provide support to a variety of organizations and causes. While they would like to respond favorably to all requests, understandably, the needs are far greater than our allocated resources and we are unable to accommodate them all.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amounts vary.

###### Focus Areas:

Children, Education, Health, Human Services

Learn more

## DanPaul Foundation Grants

The DanPaul Foundation was established to help children gain the tools they'll need in life, and teach them how to use them. Libby Moore Cornett, the Foundation's founder and president, is passionate about helping children. The Foundation aims to provide educational opportunities for teachers and parents on a variety of early childhood development topics, as well as support existing organizations dedicated to work related to youth welfare in order to make a collective impact.

###### Funding Available:

Grants of up to $15,000 are available.

###### Focus Areas:

Early Childhood, Abuse/Neglect/Domestic Violence, Education, Housing & Homelessness

Learn more

## Grace Helen Spearman Charitable Foundation

The Grace Helen Spearman Charitable Foundation honors the legacy of Ms. Spearman by providing funds to organizations and individuals to help improve the lives of the needy and less fortunate in the community. The mandate of the foundation remains consistent with Ms. Spearman’s kind and loving vision, giving to the types of organizations she supported with special emphasis in the fields of education, medical research, cultural arts and assisting the impoverished and disabled.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amount not specified.

###### Focus Areas:

Education, Research, Arts & Culture, Disabilities & Accessibility, Community Supports

Learn more

## Hearst Foundations

The Hearst Foundations are national philanthropic resources for organizations working in the fields of culture, education, health and social services. The Hearst Foundations identify and fund outstanding nonprofits to ensure that people of all backgrounds in the United States have the opportunity to build healthy, productive and satisfying lives.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amounts vary.

###### Focus Areas:

Arts & Culture, Education, Health, Social Services

Learn more

## Incyte Corporation

Incyte Corporation is a biopharmaceutical company, committed to supporting innovative, high-quality initiatives that provide healthcare professionals with evidence-based, clinically relevant, performance-based education, in order to advance healthcare practice and improve patient outcomes. Incyte supports organizations that share our commitment to ensure that students, healthcare providers, patients, caregivers, and patient advocates have access to healthcare information.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amount not specified.

###### Focus Areas:

Education, Professional Development, Workforce Development, Health

Learn more

## J.W. Couch Foundation

Jesse W. Couch lived a life dedicated to the betterment of his community. The Couch family now stewards the foundation he created to carry on his legacy of service for future generations. They believe that impact is best accomplished through partnerships with local organizations that know the people and communities they serve. They invest in and support initiatives that promote wellness and mental health and organizations seeking to provide and further education for all communities.

###### Funding Available:

Minimum grant request is $50,000.

###### Focus Areas:

Education, Health, Mental Health

Learn more

## James B. and Lois R. Archer Charitable Foundation

The James B. and Lois R. Archer Charitable Foundation was formed to provide grants to 501(c)(3) non-profit organizations, especially those who serve children and young adults. The Foundation focuses on several key program areas that aim to create lasting, positive change in communities. These areas include Arts and Culture, Civic and Public Affairs, Education, Health, and Human Services. They prioritize initiatives that promote social justice, equity, and inclusion, with a particular focus on addressing systemic challenges and supporting underserved populations.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amounts vary.

###### Focus Areas:

Arts & Culture, Education, Health, Human Services, Civic & Public Affairs

Learn more

## Jennie K. Scaife Charitable Foundation

The Jennie K. Scaife Charitable Foundation supports programs that strengthen families and address the health, safety, and welfare of women and children, including efforts that help women and children live free of violence, addiction, and abuse.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amount not specified.

###### Focus Areas:

Women & Girls, Health, Abuse/Neglect/Domestic Violence, Substance Use

Learn more

## Josiah Macy Jr. Foundation

The Josiah Macy Jr. Foundation’s President’s Grants support short-term (one year), innovative projects that advance health professions education. Funding is available for initiatives that promote diversity, equity, and belonging; strengthen interprofessional collaboration; and prepare future health professionals to navigate ethical challenges. Eligible projects should be demonstration-based, learner-centered, and situated within the clinical learning environment. Grants are awarded only to U.S. tax-exempt institutions.

###### Funding Available:

Grants of up to $25,000 are available.

###### Focus Areas:

Education, Health, Workforce Development, Health Equity

Learn more

## MrBallen Foundation

The MrBallen Foundation Honor Grant program provides financial grants to 501c3 organizations that support victims or families of victims who have been affected by a heinous crime. The Honor Grants can be used for a wide range of projects or programs. To qualify, your organization must provide services to victims and/or victims’ families of heinous, egregious, and/or accidental death or dismemberment.

###### Funding Available:

Grants of up to $25,000 are available.

###### Focus Areas:

Abuse/Neglect/Domestic Violence

Learn more

## Nathan Cummings Foundation

Nathan Cummings Foundation (NCF) is a multigenerational family foundation, rooted in the Jewish tradition of social justice. NCF is working to help create a more just, vibrant, sustainable, and democratic society. They partner with social change organizations that have creative and catalytic solutions to climate change and inequality.

###### Funding Available:

Grants range from $250,000 - $750,000.

###### Focus Areas:

Racial Justice

Learn more

## Popeyes Foundation

The Popeyes Foundation Food Love Grants program focuses on supporting nonprofits that provide food to those in need. Food Love Grants range in support of on-site feeding programs, mobile kitchens, homebound food delivery programs, out-of-school meals, and disaster-related food support. Support is directed to nonprofits that are pre-qualified and invited to apply by the Popeye's Foundation. There is no deadline, but a pre-application is required.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amounts vary.

###### Focus Areas:

Nutrition/Hunger Relief

Learn more

## Ravi and Naina Patel Foundation

The Ravi and Naina Patel Foundation support nonprofits that improve community well‑being through education, health, and social services, with a strong focus on underserved populations. Funding is available for programs in areas such as education and youth development, health and human services, community development, housing, nutrition, and healthy eating. There are no application deadlines, and organizations can apply on a rolling basis by submitting an inquiry and Form 990.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amount not specified.

###### Focus Areas:

Education, Health, Human Services, Nutrition/Hunger Relief, Housing & Homelessness

Learn more

## Roger I. and Ruth B. MacFarlane Foundation

Support to nonprofits for education, health, economic empowerment, environmental justice, and programs for women and girls. The Foundation identifies potential organizations for Category Two grants during the Spring of each year, invites proposals in early Summer, and makes decisions in the Fall.

###### Funding Available:

Grants of up to $250,000 are available.

###### Focus Areas:

Education, Health, Women & Girls

Learn more

## Russell Sage Foundation

The Russell Sage Foundation supports innovative social science research that explores cutting-edge methods, questions, and policies for the improvement of social and living conditions in the United States. The foundation dedicates itself to strengthening the methods, data, and theories of the social sciences to diagnose social problems and improve social policies. It also funds researchers at other institutions and supports programs intended to develop new generations of social scientists.

###### Funding Available:

Grants up to $200,000 are available.

###### Focus Areas:

Research, Racial Justice, Social Justice, Immigration

Learn more

## Singing for Change Charitable Foundation

Provides $1,000 to $10,000 in operating support to nonprofits helping underserved individuals and families overcome barriers to education, employment, and economic stability through programs that promote long-term self-sufficiency and community empowerment.

###### Funding Available:

Grants range from $1,000 to $10,000.

###### Focus Areas:

General Operating Support

Learn more

## Smart & Final Charitable Foundation

The Smart & Final Charitable Foundation supports community giving across health, education, hunger relief, youth programs, and disaster response. Beyond its large in-store campaigns, the Foundation nourishes the communities it serves through direct donations to local organizations, including PTAs, youth sports leagues, food pantries, emergency services providers, and more.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amount not specified.

###### Focus Areas:

Health, Education, Youth & Teens, Nutrition/Hunger Relief

Learn more

## Smart Family Fund Angel Philanthropy

The Family Fund funds early-stage non-profits with the potential for positive, scalable impact that have not yet demonstrated the efficacy needed for growth funding or a sustainable model. They offer support to nonprofits, especially first-time applicants, focused on education, youth services, community development, and social impact.

###### Funding Available:

Grants range from $25,000 to $100,000.

###### Focus Areas:

Education, Social Impact, Youth & Teens

Learn more

## Stater Bros. Charities

Stater Bros. Charities makes grants to nonprofit organizations that help strengthen the communities where Stater Bros. Markets are located. Focus areas include: children's well-being, education, health, hunger relief, pet well-being, and veterans/active duty. Their annual grant cycle is open February 1st – July 31st.

###### Funding Available:

Grants typically range from $500 to $2,500.

###### Focus Areas:

Children, Education, Health, Nutrition/Hunger Relief

Learn more

## Third Wave Fund

Third Wave Fund is an activist fund that resources youth-led, intersectional gender justice movements led by and for communities of color, advancing the power, well-being, and self-determination of BIPOC youth and women, intersex, trans, and queer communities. Through a family of grantmaking programs, Third Wave moves resources to grassroots organizing across the United States and its Territories, reaching organization regardless of 501(c)(3) status or fiscal sponsorship.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amounts vary.

###### Focus Areas:

Gender Justice, Youth & Teens, Capacity Building, Disabilities & Accessibility, General Operating Support, Justice-Impacte/Incarcerated

Learn more

## UniHealth Foundation

UniHealth Foundation provides grants to nonprofit organizations, collaboratives, and partnerships that provide direct healthcare, as well as human, and social services in underserved communities. The foundation funds projects focused on leveraging resources, social determinants of health, long-term sustainability, and organizational capacity building. Grants target the Southern California region, specifically Los Angeles and/or Orange Counties. Requests from other California counties will be considered case-by-case basis. Letters of Inquiry are accepted on a rolling basis.

###### Funding Available:

Funding amount not specified.

###### Focus Areas:

Social Determinants of Health, Social Services, Health, Human Services

Learn more

## W.K. Kellogg Foundation Grants

Supports communities, children, and families as they strengthen and create conditions that propel vulnerable children to achieve success. Funding priorities include programs focused on thriving children, working families, and building equitable communities. Submit letter of inquiry.

###### Funding Available:

​

###### Focus Areas:

Social Determinants of Health, Advancing Youth, Public Health
